### ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ FIG-MAC: A Fine-grained Inspiration Graph Empowered Multi-Agent Collaboration for Automated Scientific Hypothesis Generation

**方向**：Agent · 大语言模型 | **作者**：Siyuan Chen, Yuping Gao, Tai Huang 等 6 人 | [原文](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33563) | [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.33563v1)

<sub>英文标题：FIG-MAC: A Fine-grained Inspiration Graph Empowered Multi-Agent Collaboration for Automated Scientific Hypothesis Generation</sub>

> Automated Scientific Hypothesis Generation (ASHG) leveraging large language models (LLMs) aims to harness their strengths in knowledge integration, logical reasoning, and text generation for efficiently producing innovative, verifiable scientific hypotheses. However, existing approaches face three critical limitations: (i) insufficient fine-grained knowledge representation, as unstructured context impairs LLM comprehension and reasoning; (ii) lack of knowledge evolution traceability, constraining the generation of innovative cross-domain conjectures; (iii) absence of human-inspired and role-specialized multi-agent collaboration, failing to leverage the efficient innovation mechanisms arising from cognitive diversity and division of labor among expert roles to complete the full ASHG-evaluation loop. To address these limitations, we propose the Fine-grained Inspiration Graph-empowered Multi-Agent Collaboration framework (FIG-MAC) including: (i) Fine-grained Inspiration Graph module (FIG), to obtain fine-grained structured knowledge representations, where nodes represent academic semantic units and edges encode heuristic relationships among them; (ii) ontology-guided hybrid reasoning module, which follows the FIG ontology to construct structured reasoning paths and uses vector search to enrich them with semantic evidence, to obtain the traceable scientific conjecture paths; (iii) role-specialized Multi-Agent framework, inspired by real scientific teamwork, enabling a closed-loop process of scientific conjecture generation, screening, and validation. Experimental results demonstrate that FIG-MAC significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods on source diversity, provenance-adjusted novelty, and raw novelty.

[papers.cool / Kimi](https://papers.cool/arxiv/2611.33563)

### ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ AnyCam: Anything Can Represent Camera for Video Generation

**方向**：视频模型 | **作者**：Weijie Lyu, Zhixin Shu, Yuntian Ke 等 4 人 | [原文](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34944) | [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.34944v1)

<sub>英文标题：AnyCam: Anything Can Represent Camera for Video Generation</sub>

> Camera-controlled video generation re-renders a video along a target camera trajectory, yet moving the camera around an object, the most natural way to change the viewpoint of an object-centric scene, remains difficult. Existing methods condition either on camera parameters, which are scale-ambiguous in monocular video, or on explicit geometry such as warped point clouds or depth, which breaks down under large viewpoint changes. We present AnyCam, a method that represents the camera with an anchor object in the scene: we reconstruct it in 3D and render it along the target trajectory, a camera signal that is scale-aware, view-complete and easy to specify relative to the object. A video diffusion model transfers the content and dynamics of the source video to the new viewpoint. Its H-Concat layout aligns source and target frames in time, so the model can be distilled into AnyCam-AR, a streaming few-step generator for on-the-fly camera control. Both models are trained on paired videos with pixel-aligned anchor renderings from multi-camera videos and static-scene captures. Experiments on diverse datasets and trajectories show that AnyCam performs favorably against camera-controlled video generation and 3D reconstruction methods in camera control and visual quality.

[papers.cool / Kimi](https://papers.cool/arxiv/2611.34944)

### ⭐⭐⭐⭐ SOLVE: Sparse, Ordered Keyposes Make LLM Agents Versatile and Efficient Robot Manipulator

**方向**：Agent · 大语言模型 | **作者**：Xiang Li, Hongyu Zhou, Ya-Li Li 等 8 人 | [原文](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34750) | [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.34750v1)

<sub>英文标题：SOLVE: Sparse, Ordered Keyposes Make LLM Agents Versatile and Efficient Robot Manipulator</sub>

> LLM agents have made remarkable progress in robot manipulation, yet they suffer from expensive inference, dense step-by-step control, and tool bottlenecks. We introduce SOLVE, a training-free, versatile and efficient agent policy: a multimodal LLM agent writes a sparse, ordered sequence of 6-DoF end-effector keyposes per call, observes the result, and writes the next, while a motion planner interpolates between them. The agent decides the number and spacing of keyposes for both fast long-range motion and fine-grained manipulation. This granularity matches the sparsity of teleoperated manipulation: most demonstrations in DROID can be compressed into a few keyposes instead of dense actions. SOLVE grounds keyposes with sparse depth queries answered by noisy sources, such as multi-view triangulation, stereo, or monocular depth, and copes with that noise itself. It also improves from execution feedback, reflecting on its own rollouts and distilling a few rules per task. SOLVE reaches state-of-the-art performance of 74.5% on RoboDojo and 84.5% on RoboLab, with only $1.7 per episode on average, 12x less than previous dense-control agent policies. On a real robot, the same agent achieves an 88% success rate (75 of 85 episodes) over 16 challenging zero-shot tasks. Our project page is at https://serenos.github.io/solve-website.

[papers.cool / Kimi](https://papers.cool/arxiv/2611.34750)

### ⭐⭐⭐⭐ Agentic-Level Adversarial Purification: Revisiting Diffusion Purifiers on Native-Resolution Agentic Inputs

**方向**：Agent · 多模态 | **作者**：Yan Lin, Zhongliang Guo, Jingyu Lin 等 4 人 | [原文](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34181) | [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.34181v1)

<sub>英文标题：Agentic-Level Adversarial Purification: Revisiting Diffusion Purifiers on Native-Resolution Agentic Inputs</sub>

> Vision-language agents act on native-resolution renders of multi-element pages, where one perturbed photo or a few altered text strokes can redirect the decision. Any content provider can craft such a perturbation on the open model behind the agent, so a plug-in purifier is the natural first line of defense. Yet diffusion purifiers were designed and evaluated at the fixed resolution of the diffusion model on classifier or natural-image benchmarks, and defense evaluations for agents test JPEG, blur and noise but no diffusion purifier. We present the first study of adversarial purification at the agentic level, for an agent built on Qwen3-VL-8B: purifiers run at native resolution on multi-element inputs, face grey-box attackers who know the agent's model but not the defense, and are compared at matched clean cost. We show that treating agentic inputs as classifier inputs misleads both evaluation and design. Resizing the input to the resolution of the diffusion model, as existing purifiers do, raises the TextVQA clean cost of the Divide-and-Conquer purifier (DC) from 4.0 to 35.7 points. On web-shop pages, DC keeps 85.0% grey-box robust accuracy against web-hardened perturbations, where cheap input transformations at matched clean cost keep at most 40.0%, and this robustness comes from the global noise level: input-estimated masks and noise levels add none. These findings hold on a disjoint held-out set and on a second VLM and yield three requirements for defenses, we release the protocol.

[papers.cool / Kimi](https://papers.cool/arxiv/2611.34181)

### ⭐⭐⭐⭐ AI Buckingham: Agentic Discovery of Dimensionless Scaling Laws Across Physical Regimes

**方向**：Agent · 大语言模型 | **作者**：Ao Cheng, Lei Zhang, Guowei He | [原文](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33961) | [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.33961v1)

<sub>英文标题：AI Buckingham: Agentic Discovery of Dimensionless Scaling Laws Across Physical Regimes</sub>

> Classical dimensional analysis defines admissible dimensionless representations but does not determine which combinations capture the dominant physical dependence, whether it changes across physical regimes, or when dependent-variable rescaling is required. We present AI Buckingham, an agentic framework for autonomous dimensionless discovery from natural-language descriptions of physical problems and observational data. It couples reasoning and planning by a large language model (LLM) with predefined symbolic and numerical tools. Literature-based knowledge retrieval supports dimensional checking, gradient-based clustering identifies physical regimes, and mutual information provides an objective, reparameterization-invariant criterion for identifying dominant dimensionless groups. Explicit numerical criteria determine the group count and assess simplification. Across ten physical systems spanning fluid dynamics, magnetohydrodynamics, thermal convection and additive manufacturing, AI Buckingham recovers established similarity representations and resolves regime-dependent scalings. For the longstanding challenge of predicting turbulent drag on rough surfaces, it identifies informative reparameterizations of surface statistics that improve equivalent sand-grain roughness estimates for engineering-oriented drag modelling.

[papers.cool / Kimi](https://papers.cool/arxiv/2611.33961)

### ⭐⭐⭐⭐ Solve Vision with Code: Coding Agents Are Stronger Visual Reasoners Than Video Models

**方向**：视频模型 | **作者**：Dezhi Luo, Fengyuan Yu, Haotian Zhang 等 14 人 | [原文](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34813) | [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.34813v1)

<sub>英文标题：Solve Vision with Code: Coding Agents Are Stronger Visual Reasoners Than Video Models</sub>

> Video generation models are increasingly evaluated as visual reasoners: given a first frame and a natural-language prompt, can the model produce the video in which the specified task is carried out? The paradigm is considered advantageous because the reasoning is native to the visual medium, unfolding directly in the frames rather than through language or code. Whether this native route is in fact the more reliable path to visual reasoning has not been tested against a symbolic alternative under the same conditions. We therefore pose the same tasks, with identical inputs, output specification and evaluator, to both media: rather than denoising pixels, a coding agent writes a program that renders the answer video, which an unmodified rule-based evaluator scores. On VBVR-Pro-Bench (Xu et al., 2026), a 500-instance suite spanning five cognitive categories with symbolic ground truth, the agent receives the same first frame and prompt as the video model, with no access to ground truth, metadata or generation models, and one attempt per instance. Three closed-model agents (Codex with gpt-6-astra, Claude Code with Fable 5.1 and Gemini CLI with Gemini 3.1 Pro) score 0.923, 0.894 and 0.893, each more than 0.22 above the strongest video model. Among 23 runnable open-weight models, the best, GLM-5, reaches 0.572, above all video models except the RL-trained one. Together, we show that for tasks whose solutions can be computed from a first frame and a prompt, program synthesis is currently a substantially more reliable route to visual reasoning than video generation. We further distinguish depictive programs, which navigate the image to recover a transferable scheme, from propositional programs, which operate on hard-coded values, revealing how visual reasoning unfolds in agent-written programs and what it implies for its nature of reasoning in general.

[papers.cool / Kimi](https://papers.cool/arxiv/2611.34813)

### ⭐⭐⭐⭐ StateWeaver: Multi-Agent Coordination of Story-State Transitions for Long-Horizon Video Generation

**方向**：视频模型 · Agent | **作者**：Modi Jin, Xuying Zhang, Yijing Guo 等 9 人 | [原文](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34290) | [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.34290v1)

<sub>英文标题：StateWeaver: Multi-Agent Coordination of Story-State Transitions for Long-Horizon Video Generation</sub>

> Modern video foundation models generate high-quality short clips, but preserving long-video consistency and correctly depicting event-driven state changes remain challenging. Existing agentic workflows face these challenges because continual planning and repair accumulate context, while reference updates remain limited. We introduce StateWeaver, which combines Active States and Hierarchical Collaboration to connect review-driven visual reference updates with cross-shot state inheritance. Active States translate state discrepancies identified during review into concrete visual asset requirements that guide reference construction, validation, and regeneration, preserving identity and already-correct content while repairing target attributes. Confirmed generation results are linked to the corresponding asset states, story time, and reference versions for verification and reuse by subsequent shots. Hierarchical Collaboration isolates execution contexts by task, clip, and repair round, and uses hierarchical memory to organize this process across multiple sets. Across multiple benchmarks, StateWeaver outperforms existing methods and produces long videos with sustained cross-shot consistency under predefined and autonomously planned storyboards.

[papers.cool / Kimi](https://papers.cool/arxiv/2611.34290)

### ⭐⭐⭐⭐ VisualMontage: Manipulating the Beliefs of Vision-Language Agents with Authentic Image Sequences

**方向**：Agent · 多模态 | **作者**：Jinwei Hu, Lai Wei, Zhuoyun Li 等 6 人 | [原文](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34603) | [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.34603v1)

<sub>英文标题：VisualMontage: Manipulating the Beliefs of Vision-Language Agents with Authentic Image Sequences</sub>

> As agents based on vision-language models (VLMs) increasingly interpret social-media content, errors in their analysis may lend credibility to rumors and amplify their spread. In this paper, we study the problem of visual cognitive collusion, in which coordinated posts combine authentic images and locally faithful captions to induce VLM agents to infer and accept attacker-chosen target claims through their own reasoning. To enable systematic evaluation of this cognitive threat, we introduce a new benchmark comprising 544 target cases and 33.2K candidate images across eight application domains. We further propose VisualMontage, a multi-agent framework for simulating such attacks through narrative planning, image selection and ordering, and caption assignment under iterative review. We evaluate this threat through extensive experiments across 12 vision-language models, examining modality effects, production-team components, and downstream defenses. Using identical attack feeds across models without victim-specific optimization, we demonstrate cross-model transferability, achieving mean attack success rates of 61.93% for proprietary models and 68.71% for open-weight models. Moreover, 92.65% of generated feeds pass independent model-based validity auditing. Even after downstream review, residual attack success rates range from 35.3% to 76.7% across the evaluated configurations. Our additional controlled comparisons further reveal that captions can improve model-rated posting naturalness while reducing attack success. Together, these findings reveal a novel cognitive threat in social-media interpretation and motivate safeguards that assess how authentic posts jointly shape agents' reasoning and conclusions.

[papers.cool / Kimi](https://papers.cool/arxiv/2611.34603)

### ⭐⭐⭐⭐ Scalpel and Brush: Pruning and Targeted Weight Editing of a Small Language Model by a Single LLM Agent

**方向**：Agent · 大语言模型 | **作者**：Sergei Koltcov, Vera Ignatenko, Anton Surkov 等 4 人 | [原文](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33426) | [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.33426v1)

<sub>英文标题：Scalpel and Brush: Pruning and Targeted Weight Editing of a Small Language Model by a Single LLM Agent</sub>

> It has recently been shown that an LLM can itself edit the weights of a language model, yet it remains unclear which part of such a result is due to the agent and which part is reproduced by a random edit of the same shape, by an instruction in the prompt, or by a standard procedure without an LLM. We unify repair and pruning as sparse weight edits to optimize a metric we specify, using BLEU as an example: three tasks -- targeted weight repair ("brush"), structured MLP neuron pruning ("scalpel", zeroing pruned weights), and layer removal with repair - are assigned to a DeepSeek Harness agent powered by DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash, with up to 50 dev evaluations. We study Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B-Instruct in three independent runs per task, with final evaluation on two hidden splits. On ordinary differential equations, changing at most 0.0084% of weights raised test BLEU by 24-32% on second-order equations and by a factor of 1.8-2.5 on equations with polynomial right-hand sides. An independent substitution check showed that higher BLEU reflects notation alignment rather than improved correctness. Masks that remove 12.5-15% of MLP neurons shrink the model by 9.4-11.2% with mean BLEU loss of at most 0.01 on both hidden splits, whereas standard pruning with equal shares per layer failed the constraint at all tested shares. On seven-class GoEmotions classification, edits raise macro-F1 from 0.100 to 0.396-0.451 on test and from 0.175 to 0.455-0.476 on holdout. A ridge-based classifier written into agent-selected weight rows yields macro-F1 of 0.424 and 0.464. Pruning search with the same evaluation limit removes 15.9% of neurons versus 14-21.4% for agent runs passing the hidden check. Standard procedures without an LLM reproduce much of the performance once given the location and form of intervention found by the agent; a comparison of agentic and non-agentic search remains open.

[papers.cool / Kimi](https://papers.cool/arxiv/2611.33426)

### ⭐⭐⭐⭐ What Must LLM Agents Communicate? From Information Loss to Communication Failures

**方向**：Agent · 大语言模型 | **作者**：Yi-Yun Chu, Xiaobin Shen | [原文](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33318) | [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.33318v1)

<sub>英文标题：What Must LLM Agents Communicate? From Information Loss to Communication Failures</sub>

> When evidence is distributed across LLM agents, a team may collectively possess all the information needed for a decision yet fail to use it effectively. What must agents communicate for accurate joint predictions, and when does interaction help? We study three requirements for effective communication: sufficiency of the information conveyed, selection of relevant evidence, and utilization of received information. Drawing on information theory and communication complexity, we develop a framework that combines theoretical benchmarks with controlled experiments and message-level analyses. We find that local probability reports can discard essential information, with theoretical information loss closely matching observed performance penalties. Query-first interaction improves predictions when relevant evidence depends on a private query, but compact one-shot representations can eliminate this advantage. Even when sufficient information is communicated, agents may fail to select or use it correctly. Together, these findings distinguish fundamental communication limits from failures in how LLM agents communicate and use information.

[papers.cool / Kimi](https://papers.cool/arxiv/2611.33318)

### ⭐⭐⭐⭐ JetDiffusion: Pushing the Speed Limits of Video Generation with NVFP4 Sparse Attention

**方向**：视频模型 · 系统与基础设施 | **作者**：Yulun Wu, Zihan Min, Minshen Zhang 等 12 人 | [原文](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33268) | [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.33268v1)

<sub>英文标题：JetDiffusion: Pushing the Speed Limits of Video Generation with NVFP4 Sparse Attention</sub>

> Attention over long sequences is a major computational bottleneck in video generation, which can be alleviated through sparsity and low-bit quantization. We propose JetDiffusion, a video generation system built on trainable Video Sparse Quantized Attention (VSQA), with custom inference kernels that combine native NVFP4 computation and kernel fusion. At 90% sparsity, VSQA accelerates attention by 8-29x compared with FlashAttention on different hardwares. To further accelerate video generation, we combine VSQA with NVFP4 linear quantization and step distillation. Although each technique can individually preserve generation quality, jointly adapting to all three remains challenging, particularly under aggressive NVFP4 quantization. Through empirical studies, we develop an effective staged training recipe that enables stable adaptation to quantization, sparsity, and few-step denoising. On Wan2.1-14B, the resulting model achieves 273x end-to-end video generation speedup on the RTX 5090 while maintaining comparable video quality.

[papers.cool / Kimi](https://papers.cool/arxiv/2611.33268)

### ⭐⭐⭐⭐ Diffusion Steering via Adaptive Reward Estimation and Resampling

**方向**：图像编辑 · 多模态 | **作者**：Abinitha Gourabathina, Sana Tonekaboni, Marzyeh Ghassemi 等 4 人 | [原文](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34839) | [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.34839v1)

<sub>英文标题：Diffusion Steering via Adaptive Reward Estimation and Resampling</sub>

> Text-to-image diffusion models can generate images that satisfy complex prompts, yet often produce images that omit requested objects, attributes, or relationships. We ask if models can more reliably generate text-aligned images by redistributing probability toward their more successful outcomes. We propose TrailMix, a training-free method that uses the base model's distribution of possible outcomes to adaptively guide generation toward better text alignment. During denoising, TrailMix generates inexpensive lookahead images from parallel trajectories (trails) and scores their alignment with the prompt using an external judge. The resulting distribution of reward scores allows us to estimate how strongly to steer generation toward a desired alignment level. We use these estimates to reweight and resample trajectories, favoring more promising outcomes. Across three text-to-image benchmarks, TrailMix outperforms existing baselines, with particularly strong gains on rare multi-object compositions. We further extend TrailMix to targeted image editing and introduce SEAM-Bench (Semantic Editing and Attribute Maintenance), a systematic benchmark evaluating whether semantic edits preserve other image content. Experiments on SEAM-Bench and existing benchmark PIE-Bench demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach for controlled image editing. Our results show how a model's own possible outcomes can inform reliable and controllable generation.

[papers.cool / Kimi](https://papers.cool/arxiv/2611.34839)

### ⭐⭐⭐⭐ SPARC: Geometry-Guided Attention Pruning for Long-Horizon Camera-Controlled Video Generation

**方向**：视频模型 | **作者**：Zesong Yang, Weikai Chen, Liyuan Cui 等 14 人 | [原文](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34064) | [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.34064v1)

<sub>英文标题：SPARC: Geometry-Guided Attention Pruning for Long-Horizon Camera-Controlled Video Generation</sub>

> We present SPARC, a geometry-guided attention pruning framework that turns the native self-attention of pretrained video diffusion transformers into a unified mechanism for camera control and persistent scene memory. Our key insight is that attention connectivity can simultaneously serve as geometric conditioning for camera control and spatial addressing for historical memory retrieval. Motivated by the asymmetric contextual requirements of reconstructing observed content and synthesizing novel regions, SPARC restricts observed tokens to geometrically corresponding historical tokens and local spatiotemporal context, while preserving dense attention for unobserved regions. Correspondence-aware attention biasing and order-invariant historical positioning further enable stable retrieval across long temporal gaps. Without introducing additional learnable control or memory modules, SPARC achieves precise camera adherence and faithful scene revisitation while substantially reducing attention computation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of aggressive attention pruning in improving camera adherence, revisit consistency, and visual fidelity, with an inference speedup of over 50x when combined with four-step distillation. Project page is available at https://zju3dv.github.io/SPARC.

[papers.cool / Kimi](https://papers.cool/arxiv/2611.34064)

### ⭐⭐⭐⭐ DualCast: Dual-Image Conditioned Diffusion Transformers for Digital Human E-Commerce Video Generation

**方向**：视频模型 | **作者**：Lifan Jiang, Yifan Huang, Jixuan Zhang 等 8 人 | [原文](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33734) | [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.33734v1)

<sub>英文标题：DualCast: Dual-Image Conditioned Diffusion Transformers for Digital Human E-Commerce Video Generation</sub>

> Recent advances in world models for game interaction and control have increased the demand for large-scale gameplay videos paired with camera trajectories. However, capturing synchronized camera trajectories often requires game-specific pipelines, limiting scalability. We therefore propose GameWeaver, a unified diffusion framework that automatically produces paired video--camera data through three complementary tasks: camera pose estimation from observed video, video generation along a prescribed camera trajectory, and joint video--camera generation from a reference image and text. First, each tipxel stores the normalized 3D coordinates of a pixel ray's endpoint in a reference coordinate frame. The resulting three-channel tipxel fields and RGB frames share the pretrained video model's VAE. Next, a fully trainable dual-stream Transformer exchanges appearance and geometry features through bidirectional gated attention and conditions both streams on shared text features. Finally, deterministic fitting recovers camera intrinsics and normalized trajectories from generated tipxel fields. In our evaluation tasks, GameWeaver achieves a normalized translation MSE of 0.0029 for camera control; for joint generation, it scores 85.48 out of 100 on instruction adherence versus 64.71 for the best cascade on that metric, validating the feasibility of the proposed approach.

[papers.cool / Kimi](https://papers.cool/arxiv/2611.33734)

### ⭐⭐⭐⭐ From Telemetry to Intelligence: A Data-Centric Survey of O-RAN

**方向**：数据工程 | **作者**：Samara Mayhoub, Zhengjia Xu, Tareq M. Shami 等 8 人 | [原文](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34262) | [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.34262v1)

<sub>英文标题：From Telemetry to Intelligence: A Data-Centric Survey of O-RAN</sub>

> The Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) has emerged as a new architecture for enabling programmability, intelligence, automation, and multi-vendor interoperability in the wireless domain of public telecommunications networks. Artificial Intelligence/Machine Learning (AI/ML), and more recently Agentic AI, are considered key enablers of automation and intelligentization in O-RAN. However, the effectiveness of such systems is constrained by the availability, quality, and structure of O-RAN data. In this survey, we present a data-centric perspective of O-RAN, focusing on how data support rule-based, AI/ML-driven, and emerging Agentic AI applications across various timescale control loops. We analyze O-RAN data along multiple dimensions, including source, accessibility, abstraction level, and application. We further identify key data challenges, and discuss their implications for intelligent control. The overarching objective of this work is to present the first comprehensive review of the data-centric perspective on intelligent applications in O-RAN. To this end, it introduces a unified framework for understanding the role of O-RAN data in enabling network intelligence and automation.

[papers.cool / Kimi](https://papers.cool/arxiv/2611.34262)

<details><summary>Kimi 深度解读（点击展开）</summary>

**Q1. 这篇论文试图解决什么问题？**

该论文旨在解决**O-RAN（开放无线接入网络）领域中智能应用与底层数据基础之间存在的系统性认知差距**。具体而言，其核心针对以下三方面问题：

**1. 现有研究过度聚焦算法与架构，忽视数据基础**
当前O-RAN研究主要集中在标准化、架构实现、安全漏洞以及AI/ML算法创新等层面，但缺乏对支撑这些智能系统的**数据本身**的系统性审视。AI/ML乃至Agentic AI应用的有效性本质上受限于O-RAN数据的可用性、质量、结构与暴露方式，而现有文献并未提供一份全面的、以数据为中心的综述来梳理这些数据的生命周期与依赖关系。

**2. Agentic AI范式带来的异构数据需求未被充分连接**
随着Agentic AI（基于大语言模型或多智能体的自主系统）成为O-RAN的新兴趋势，网络智能不再仅依赖传统的数值型遥测（如KPM）进行模型推理。智能体还需要消费**异构的语义数据**，包括自然语言意图、操作日志、策略文档、工具描述、历史决策轨迹及环境反馈。该论文指出，现有研究未能充分阐明这种新型智能范式对O-RAN数据暴露、抽象级别和可信性提出的新要求。

**3. O-RAN数据环境的复杂性与碎片化**
O-RAN数据天然具有高度异构性：它产生于解耦的多厂商组件（如O-RU、O-DU、O-CU、RIC、SMO），通过多种开放/专有接口暴露，呈现为从原始I/Q样本到聚合指标、再到高层策略的多种抽象级别，并被部署在不同时标（实时、近实时、非实时）控制环上的各类应用（xApps、rApps、dApps）所消费。这种碎片化使得理解“数据如何支撑智能”变得极为困难。

为解决上述问题，该论文提出了**首个面向O-RAN智能应用的数据中心视角综述**，并构建了一个统一框架，从**数据来源、可访问性、抽象级别、应用用途与挑战**五个互补维度对O-RAN数据进行系统化组织。通过该框架，论文将传统规则型、AI/ML驱动型及新兴的Agentic AI应用映射到其跨时标控制环的具体数据依赖上，并识别了影响智能控制的关键数据瓶颈（如数据稀缺、异构性、隐私安全、时效性与质量等），从而为构建更可靠、可扩展和自主的O-RAN智能系统奠定数据层面的理论基础。

**Q2. 有哪些相关研究？**

根据论文第I节（Introduction）及全文脉络，已有相关研究主要分布于以下五个方向：

### 1. O-RAN 架构、标准化与部署实现
- **标准化组织**：O-RAN Alliance [3] 主导了 O-RAN 架构与接口规范；3GPP [4] 定义了相关的 5G/6G 核心标准。
- **开源实现**：srsRAN [5] 与 OpenAirInterface (OAI) [6] 提供了软件协议栈实现。
- **架构与部署教程**：Polese 等 [1] 对 O-RAN 架构、接口、算法与安全进行了系统性综述；Herrera 等 [7] 提供了从仿真、模拟到真实测试床的部署教程；Alam 等 [2] 聚焦于切片感知架构与部署选项。

### 2. O-RAN 安全与隐私威胁研究
- **总体安全框架**：Liyanage 等 [20] 综述了 O-RAN 的安全与隐私威胁、漏洞及缓解措施；Abdalla 与 Marojevic [10] 提出了端到端 O-RAN 安全架构与威胁面分析；其他研究还涉及威胁建模、开放接口攻击实验等 [11]–[13]。
- **接口与应用安全**：Groen 等 [21] 专门分析了 E2 与 O-FH 开放接口的安全问题；Kakani 等 [22] 评估了 xApp 的架构及其安全漏洞。
- **AI/ML 管道安全**：近期工作开始关注数据投毒 [205]–[206]、对抗样本、后门攻击 [208]、恶意 xApp/rApp 以及未授权控制动作等风险。

### 3. 网络管理应用与智能控制算法
该方向涵盖部署在 Non-RT RIC、Near-RT RIC 及 O-DU/O-CU 节点的各类应用，按智能程度可细分为：
- **传统统计与优化方法**：基于聚类、相关性分析、启发式优化或整数规划的 rApp/xApp/dApp，用于配置冲突检测 [132]、能效管理 [133]、[135]、信令风暴检测 [84]、QoE 驱动的资源分配 [134] 及 RT 频谱共享 [120]。
- **AI/ML 驱动应用**：
  - **rApp**：利用 LSTM、Random Forest、DQN-MARL 等模型实现干扰降低 [139]、基站节能 [138] 与 RAN 切片容量共享 [140]。
  - **xApp**：基于 CNN、LSTM、GNN、联邦学习等，实现流量切片分类 [106]、异常与攻击检测 [142]、[144]、[105]、动态切片与资源优化 [143]、rogue 基站检测 [146]、智能连接管理 [147] 及 QoS 驱动的 PRB 优化 [149]。
  - **dApp**：部署于 O-DU/O-CU 近端，利用轻量级 CNN 进行 RT 频谱分类 [112]、干扰检测 [150]、[151] 及 RT 控制 [27]。
  - **分层/闭环架构**：结合 Non-RT 与 Near-RT 的预测性切片资源分配 [152]–[153]、节能与流量疏导协调 [154]、切换优化 [155]、多智能体强化学习资源管理 [156]、多层 DDoS 防御 [157] 等。
- **Agentic AI 与 LLM 应用**（新兴方向）：近年来出现将大语言模型（LLM）与多智能体系统引入 O-RAN 的研究，包括单智能体意图驱动控制（如 LLM-xApp [114]、LLM-hRIC [164]）；多智能体协作框架（如 Bimo 等 [113]、[165]、MX-AI [167]、MobiLLM [168]、ORION [197]）；以及跨实时/

**Q3. 论文如何解决这个问题？**

该论文通过构建一个**统一的数据中心框架（Data-Centric Framework）**，系统性地刻画、分类与映射O-RAN数据景观，从而弥合智能应用与底层数据基础之间的认知差距。具体的解决路径可分为以下六个层面：

### 1. 提出五维互补的数据中心框架
为组织O-RAN中高度异构的数据，论文引入了包含五个互补视角的分析框架：
- **来源视角（Source View）**：界定数据在O-RAN生态系统中的产生位置，涵盖无线接入组件、RAN功能、管理系统、核心网、外部系统、O-RAN应用及Agentic AI交互。
- **可访问性视角（Accessibility View）**：明确数据通过何种接口路径被获取，包括原生O-RAN接口（E2、A1、O1、O2、E3、R1）、3GPP接口（Uu、F1、E1、N2/N3、Xn）以及新兴的Agentic AI互操作接口（MCP、ACP、A2A、ANP）。
- **抽象级别视角（Abstraction View）**：刻画数据从原始形态到可消费形态的转化层次，划分为原始数据、聚合与统计数据、衍生与增强数据、以及智能衍生数据四个层级。
- **应用视角（Application View）**：依据底层方法将应用分为传统统计应用、AI/ML驱动应用和Agentic AI应用，并分析各类应用对数据类型与时标控制环的差异化需求。
- **挑战视角（Challenge View）**：识别影响数据全生命周期的关键瓶颈，包括安全性、隐私性、稀缺性、异构性、质量、时效性与预处理复杂度。

### 2. 系统分类O-RAN数据来源与类型
论文对O-RAN数据进行了全面的类型学梳理，填补了以往文献仅零散提及数据类型的空白：
- **无线层数据**：$I/Q$ 样本、频谱图（Spectrograms）、参考信号（CSI-RS、DM-RS、SRS）等原始射频观测。
- **细粒度RAN遥测**：跨PHY、MAC、RLC、PDCP、SDAP、RRC各协议层的标准化测量指标。
- **聚合KPM数据**：通过E2接口由E2节点上报的UE级、节点级、小区级、切片级及QoS级关键性能测量。
- **管理面与历史数据**：通过O1接口收集的性能管理（PM）、故障管理（FM）和配置管理（CM）数据。
- **控制面与信令数据**：RRC连接建立、随机接入、切换与移动性事件等信令流程数据。
- **跨域数据**：A1接口传递的策略（Policies）与富化信息（EI）、核心网会话/服务上下文、用户面流量数据。
- **智能衍生数据**：xApp、rApp、dApp产生的控制决策、预测、异常评分等二次数据。
- **用户意图数据**：自然语言或结构化（JSON/YAML）的操作意图，作为Agentic AI的新型输入。

### 3. 映射应用与数据依赖至跨时标控制环
论文将不同智能程度的应用映射到其具体的数据依赖与控制时标，明确了“何种数据在何处被何种应用消费”：
- **非实时控制环（Non-RT, >1 s）**：rApp消费网络级管理数据、历史观测与策略，通过A1接口向Near-RT RIC提供高层指导。
- **近实时控制环（Near-RT, 10 ms–1 s）**：xApp通过E2接口消费KPM与细粒度遥测，执行资源管理与优化决策。
- **实时控制环（RT, <10 ms）**：dApp部署于O-DU/O-CU节点，直接处理本地$I/Q$样本、主机遥测与用户面数据，实现超低延迟的本地智能。
- **分层闭环架构**：展示rApp、xApp、dApp如何通过A1、E2及本地接口形成层级化的智能闭环，支撑从策略制定到实时执行的纵向协同。

### 4. 引入Agentic AI的数据需求分析
针对新兴的Agentic AI范式，论文特别阐释了其对O-RAN数据生态的新要求：智能体不仅需要传统数值遥测，还需消费**异构语义数据**，包括自然语言意图、操作日志、工具描述、标准文档、历史决策记录及环境反馈。论文进一步梳理了支撑智能体协作的互操作接口（如MCP、A2A），将Agentic AI的数据需求纳入统一的分析框架。

### 5. 识别关键数据挑战及其对智能控制的影响
论文系统性地诊断了制约O-RAN智能应用可靠性的数据层面瓶颈：
- **数据安全**：KPM投毒、UE测量值篡改、训练数据污染、后门攻击及对抗样本等威胁。
- **数据隐私**：开放接口与多厂商环境下的UE身份、位置与流量模式泄露风险，以及AI模型反向推断风险。
- **数据稀缺**：缺乏公开大规模数据集、现网配置探索受限、罕见事件采样不足及隐私法规导致的共享限制。
- **数据异构与质量**：多厂商数据格式差异、非独立同分布（non-IID）数据、噪声、时延与不一致性对AI模型的干扰。

### 6. 指明未来研究方向
基于上述分析，论文提出需优先突破的研究方向：标准化与语义对齐的数据模型、可复用的低延迟预处理流水线、隐私保护的数据共享机制、对抗数据污染的弹性智能闭环，以及真实测试床验证，为后续从“以算法为中心”转向“以数据为中心”的O-RAN研究提供路线图。

**Q4. 论文做了哪些实验？**

该论文是一篇**综述性（Survey）研究**，其核心贡献在于提出一个统一的数据中心框架并对现有文献进行系统性分类与梳理，**论文作者本身并未进行新的实验验证**。

论文中的“实验”或“实现”相关内容主要体现在对**已有研究的回顾与总结**上。具体而言，作者在**第七节（Application View of O-RAN Data）**中，通过四个表格（Table VI–IX）总结了近年来O-RAN领域具有代表性的应用实现，这些实验/实现均来自引用的文献，按其智能程度与部署位置可归纳如下：

### 1. 传统统计/启发式应用（Table VI）
论文总结了多项已实现的 rApp/xApp/dApp，例如：
- **rApp**：Armstrong 等 [132] 利用 KPM 与配置管理数据，通过聚类与皮尔逊相关性检测配置冲突；Levis 等 [133] 实现了 SLEEPY-rApp，用于 MEC 场景下的能效感知资源编排。
- **xApp**：Hoffmann 等 [84] 在真实测试床上开发了信令风暴检测 xApp，基于随机接入响应与 RRC 建立请求消息的统计画像；Agarwal 等 [134] 实现了 QoE 驱动的用户关联与功率分配 xApp。
- **dApp**：Crespo 等 [107] 在 O-DU 上部署了基于主机遥测的轻量级 CPU 管理 dApp；Gangula 等 [120] 实现了基于 $I/Q$ 样本能量门限的实时频谱共享 dApp。

### 2. AI/ML 驱动应用（Table VII）
论文回顾了大量基于机器学习的实验实现，涵盖：
- **rApp**：Tran 等 [139] 在真实 5G 部署中测试了多种 ML/DL 模型（SVM、Random Forest、XGBoost、DNN、LSTM）以降低 RAN 干扰；Kim 等 [138] 开发了基于 LSTM 的网络节能 rApp；Vilà 等 [140] 实现了基于 DQN-MARL 的切片容量共享 rApp。
- **xApp**：Groen 等 [106] 开发了基于 CNN 的流量切片分类工具 TRACTOR；Mayhoub 等 [144] 与 Huang 等 [146] 分别实现了基于 BiDLSTM 的信令风暴检测和基于 Random Forest 的伪基站检测 xApp；Orhan 等 [147] 利用 GNN 与深度 Q 学习优化用户-小区关联。
- **dApp**：Olimpieri 等 [112] 提出了 LibIQ，一种部署在 O-DU 近端、基于 CNN 的实时频谱分类 dApp；Lacava 等 [27] 在 OAI 实验平台上验证了基于 E3 接口的 dApp 架构，实现了亚毫秒级控制闭环；Neasamoni Santhi 等 [151] 设计了基于 GPU 加速 CNN 的实时上行干扰检测 dApp。
- **分层/闭环架构**：Filali 等 [152] 与 Thaliath 等 [153] 分别展示了结合 Non-RT RIC（离线训练/预测）与 Near-RT RIC（在线控制）的深度强化学习与 LSTM 预测性资源调配框架；Akman 等 [154] 在多厂商互操作测试床上验证了节能与流量疏导的 rApp/xApp 协同。

### 3. Agentic AI 应用（Table VIII & IX）
论文还总结了新兴的 Agentic AI 实验原型：
- **单智能体**：Wu 等 [114] 提出了 LLM-xApp，利用 GPT-4 通过上下文学习动态生成 PRB 分配决策；Bao 等 [164] 实现了 LLM-hRIC，结合大语言模型（Non-RT 策略生成）与 RL-based xApp（Near-RT 执行）。
- **多智能体（同层）**：Bimo 等 [113]、[165] 开发了基于 LLM 的意图驱动 RAN 管理框架与合同化智能体切片框架（CAIF）；Chatzistefanidis 等 [167] 部署了 MX-AI 平台，通过多智能体 LLM 实现闭环 RAN 重配置；Sharma 等 [168] 提出了 MobiLLM，一种用于威胁缓解的多智能体 rApp 框架。
- **分层多智能体（跨时标）**：Elkael 等 [115] 提出了 AgentRAN，在 Non-RT/Near-RT/RT 三层部署 LLM 智能体，通过 A2A/MCP 协调实现跨域自主控制；Navidan 等 [161] 设计了基于 WPFM 的 dApp、SLM-based xApp 与 LLM-based rApp 的分层多尺度智能体架构；Shokouhi 等 [183] 在 cell-free O-RAN 场景中实现了主管 rApp 与多个 xApp/dApp 的协同优化。

### 总结
该论文**并未报告原创的实验结果**，其方法论价值在于通过**文献综合分析与框架构建**，将上述分散在不同文献中的实验实现按照“数据来源—接口可访问性—抽象级别—应用类型—面临挑战”的维度进行了重新组织与映射，从而首次从数据中心视角揭示了 O-RAN 智能应用背后的数据依赖全景。

**Q5. 有什么可以进一步探索的点？**

基于论文识别的数据挑战与结论，以下方向值得进一步探索：

### 1. 标准化与语义对齐的数据模型
O-RAN 数据在跨厂商、跨接口、跨协议层场景中呈现严重的语义碎片化。未来需研究：
- **统一数据本体（Ontology）与元数据标准**：为 $I/Q$ 样本、KPM、控制面信令、管理面数据及 Agentic AI 的意图数据定义通用的语义描述、时间戳格式与数据溯源（Provenance）机制。
- **跨接口数据映射规范**：建立 E2、A1、O1、O2 等接口间自动化的语义转换与对齐协议，减少多 vendor 部署中的数据不一致性。

### 2. 公开数据集与合成数据生成
数据稀缺是制约 AI/ML 与 Agentic AI 应用的关键瓶颈：
- **大规模公开 O-RAN 数据集建设**：整合无线层测量（RSRP/RSRQ/SINR）、MAC/RLC/PDCP 层指标、KPM、信令日志及用户面流量，覆盖正常与异常（攻击、故障、拥塞）场景。
- **合成数据与数字孪生**：利用条件生成对抗网络（CTGAN）、扩散模型或高精度 O-RAN 数字孪生（如 Colosseum）生成 rare-event 样本，缓解现网数据获取受限与隐私法规约束。
- **少样本与迁移学习**：针对新服务或新场景下的数据不足，探索基于元学习（Meta-Learning）与域自适应（Domain Adaptation）的跨场景模型迁移。

### 3. 数据安全与可信 AI
开放架构放大了数据投毒与对抗攻击面：
- **KPM 与 UE 测量值投毒检测**：设计轻量级异常检测器（如基于 LSTM 或统计假设检验），在 xApp/rApp 消费前验证遥测数据的完整性。
- **训练管道完整性保护**：研究面向 rApp/xApp 模型的供应链安全机制，包括训练数据审计、模型签名与后门触发器检测。
- **弹性控制闭环**：构建对 poisoned/manipulated 数据具有鲁棒性的 Agentic AI 决策框架，结合输入置信度评估与保守降级策略（Conservative Fallback）。

### 4. 数据隐私保护机制
多 stakeholder 环境下的数据共享与隐私存在张力：
- **隐私增强技术（PETs）**：在联邦学习（FL）场景中集成差分隐私（Differential Privacy）、安全聚合（Secure Aggregation）与同态加密，防止梯度泄露与成员推断攻击。
- **零信任数据访问**：将零信任架构（Zero-Trust Architecture）延伸至 RIC 数据库与接口，实现细粒度的数据加密存储、隐私保护推理（Privacy-Preserving Inference）及动态访问控制。
- **用户面数据脱敏**：研究针对 $I/Q$ 样本、频谱图及信令数据的自动化匿名化与假名化技术，平衡数据分析价值与 UE 隐私。

### 5. 自动化数据预处理与质量评估流水线
原始 O-RAN 数据噪声大、格式异构、时标不一：
- **低延迟数据清洗与对齐**：开发面向 RT/Near-RT 控制环的流式预处理流水线，支持噪声滤波、缺失值填补、时间同步与多源数据融合。
- **数据质量实时监控**：定义并量化数据质量指标（如新鲜度、完整性、一致性、可信度），设计嵌入 RIC 的质量评估模块，在数据进入 AI/ML 推理前进行 gate-keeping。
- **特征工程自动化**：利用 AutoML 或 LLM 驱动的特征选择，从海量 PHY/MAC/RLC 指标中自动提取与目标任务最相关的低冗余特征集。

### 6. Agentic AI 的数据基础设施
Agentic AI 对数据类型与交互模式提出了全新要求：
- **异构数据统一表征**：研究如何将自然语言意图、JSON/YAML 策略、实时 KPM 流、操作日志与 3GPP/O-RAN 标准文档统一编码为 LLM/SLM 可消费的上下文（Context）。
- **工具与接口的语义封装**：通过 Model Context Protocol（MCP）等机制，将 O-RAN 的 E2 控制、A1 策略、O1 配置等能力封装为 LLM Agent 可调用的标准工具（Tools），并验证其安全性与原子性。
- **多智能体数据协同**：探索跨 Non-RT/Near-RT/RT 层级的智能体间数据共享协议，解决分层决策中的信息瓶颈与语义歧义。

### 7. 跨域数据融合与异构性处理
O-RAN 数据天然跨射频、协议、核心网与管理域：
- **多模态数据融合架构**：联合处理 $I/Q$ 样本（信号域）、KPM（网络域）、流量数据（用户域）与外部 EI（地理/业务域），研究多模态学习（Multimodal Learning）与跨域注意力机制。
- **Non-IID 数据下的分布式学习**：针对 O-DU/O-CU 本地数据分布差异，改进联邦学习聚合策略（如个性化联邦学习），提升模型在多 vendor、多场景下的泛化能力。

### 8. 实时数据时效性保障
控制环的延迟要求对数据新鲜度提出硬性约束：
- **自适应采样与报告机制**：设计基于网络状态动态调整 KPM 上报周期与粒度的机制，避免 E2/O1 接口拥塞同时保证关键事件的捕获。
- **边缘-云数据分级缓存**：在 O-DU、Near-RT RIC、Non-RT RIC 间构建分层数据缓存与预取策略，满足不同控制环对数据时效性的差异化需求。

### 9. 数字孪生驱动的数据闭环验证
- **数据-模型-控制联合仿真**：在数字孪生平台中注入真实或合成的 O-RAN 数据流，闭环验证 AI/Agentic 控制策略在数据延迟、丢失、污染场景下的稳健性。
- **人在回路中的数据审计**：对于高影响控制操作（如大规模小区关断、安全策略更新），建立基于数据可视化与可解释 AI 的人类监督接口，确保数据驱动决策的可审计性。

**Q6. 总结一下论文的主要内容**

该论文题为《From Telemetry to Intelligence: A Data-Centric Survey of O-RAN》，提出了**首个面向O-RAN智能应用的数据中心视角综述**。以下从八个方面总结其主要内容：

### 1. 研究背景与动机
论文指出，O-RAN（开放无线接入网络）通过解耦、虚拟化、开放接口和智能控制四大原则，实现了多厂商互操作与可编程性。现有研究多聚焦于架构标准化、安全漏洞或AI/ML算法创新，却**忽视了支撑这些智能系统的底层数据基础**。随着Agentic AI（基于大语言模型和多智能体的自主系统）的兴起，O-RAN应用不仅需要传统数值遥测，还需消费异构语义数据（如意图、日志、策略、工具描述等），这使得**数据可用性、质量、抽象级别与可信度**成为制约网络智能的关键瓶颈。

### 2. 五维数据中心框架（核心贡献）
为系统组织O-RAN的异构数据景观，论文提出了一个统一的**五维数据中心框架**：
- **来源视角（Source View）**：数据在何处产生。
- **可访问性视角（Accessibility View）**：数据通过何种接口路径暴露。
- **抽象级别视角（Abstraction View）**：数据经何种加工程度后被消费。
- **应用视角（Application View）**：数据被何种类型应用（传统统计、AI/ML、Agentic AI）消费。
- **挑战视角（Challenge View）**：数据全生命周期面临何种瓶颈。

### 3. 数据来源与类型（Source View）
论文系统分类了O-RAN中的八类数据：
- **无线层数据**：$I/Q$ 样本、频谱图（Spectrograms）、参考信号（CSI-RS、SRS等）。
- **细粒度RAN遥测**：跨PHY、MAC、RLC、PDCP、SDAP、RRC各协议层的原始测量指标（如RSRP、SINR、PRB利用率、RLC重传率等）。
- **聚合KPM数据**：通过E2接口上报的UE级、节点级、小区级、切片级及QoS级关键性能测量。
- **管理面与历史数据**：通过O1接口收集的性能管理（PM）、故障管理（FM）和配置管理（CM）数据。
- **控制面与信令数据**：RRC建立、随机接入、切换、移动性事件及NAS消息。
- **跨域数据**：A1接口的策略（Policies）与富化信息（EI）、核心网会话/服务上下文、用户面流量数据（包级、流级、连接级、主机级）。
- **智能衍生数据**：xApp、rApp、dApp输出的控制决策、预测、异常评分等。
- **用户意图数据**：自然语言或结构化（JSON/YAML）的操作目标，作为Agentic AI的新型输入。

### 4. 数据可访问性与接口（Accessibility View）
论文梳理了三类数据访问机制：
- **O-RAN原生接口**：E2（Near-RT RIC与E2节点间，用于KPM上报与控制）、A1（Non-RT RIC向Near-RT RIC下发策略/EI）、O1（SMO管理面数据收集）、O2（云资源管理）、E3（dApp与O-DU/O-CU的实时交互）、R1（rApp与Non-RT RIC）。
- **3GPP接口**：Uu、F1-C/U、E1、NG-C/N2、NG-U/N3、Xn等，承载无线、控制面、用户面及核心网数据。
- **Agentic AI互操作接口**：MCP（LLM与工具/数据源连接）、ACP（异构智能体通信）、A2A（智能体间协作）、ANP（去中心化点对点智能体网络）。

### 5. 数据抽象级别（Abstraction View）
数据按加工程度分为四个层级：
- **原始数据**：未处理的 $I/Q$ 样本、日志、pcap等，保真度高但存储传输成本大。
- **聚合与统计数据**：时间/空间窗口汇总后的KPM、统计分布等，紧凑但可能掩盖瞬态异常。
- **衍生与增强数据**：经特征工程计算的指标（如拥塞分数）或融合外部上下文后的增强数据。
- **智能衍生数据**：AI/ML或Agentic系统输出的预测、分类、控制决策，需关注其置信度与时效性。

### 6. 应用映射与分类（Application View）
论文按智能程度与部署位置，将应用及其数据依赖映射至不同时标控制环：
- **统计/启发式应用**：基于聚类、优化或阈值规则，部署于rApp（>1 s）、xApp（10 ms–1 s）或dApp（<10 ms）。
- **AI/ML驱动应用**：利用LSTM、CNN、GNN、强化学习等，消费KPM、PHY测量、流量数据或 $I/Q$ 样本，实现节能、切片、入侵检测、干扰管理等功能。
- **Agentic AI应用**：基于LLM/多智能体，消费自然语言意图、遥测、日志与知识库，通过MCP/A2A等接口实现跨层自主控制。论文进一步将其分为单智能体、同层多智能体及跨Non-RT/Near-RT/RT的**分层多智能体架构**。

### 7. 关键数据挑战（Challenge View）
论文识别了制约O-RAN智能的六大数据挑战：
- **数据安全**：KPM投毒、UE测量值篡改、训练数据污染、后门攻击、对抗样本及恶意xApp/rApp。
- **数据隐私**：开放接口与多厂商环境导致UE身份/位置/流量模式泄露风险，以及AI模型的成员推断与模型反转攻击。
- **数据稀缺**：缺乏公开大规模数据集、现网配置探索受限、罕见事件样本不足、隐私法规限制数据共享。
- **数据异构性**：跨组件、跨厂商、跨接口的数据在格式、语义、采样率上差异巨大，导致非独立同分布（non-IID）问题。
- **数据质量**：噪声、时延（数据新鲜度不足）及多源数据不一致。
- **数据预处理**：缺乏标准化、自动化的清洗、对齐、特征提取与时标同步流水线。

### 8. 结论与未来方向
论文结论强调，O-RAN的智能化演进必须从“以算法为中心”转向**“以数据为中心”**。未来研究方向包括：
- 建立**标准化、语义对齐的数据模型与元数据规范**；
- 构建**公开数据集、合成数据生成与隐私保护共享机制**；
- 开发**自动化、低延迟的数据预处理与质量评估流水线**；
- 设计**对抗数据污染的弹性智能闭环**与可审计的Agentic AI控制；
- 在**真实测试床**中验证数据中心框架的有效性。
